    def _load_source(root, decl_dir, name, spec, memo):
        if not isinstance(spec, dict):
            raise _reject("pdk_decl_shape", where="sources." + name)
        fmt = spec.get("format")
        allowed = dict(_SOURCE_KEYS).get(fmt)
        if allowed is None:
            raise _reject("pdk_source_format_unknown", source=name, format=fmt)
        _require_keys("sources." + name, spec, allowed, tuple(k for k in allowed if k not in _OPTIONAL_SOURCE_KEYS))
        units_m = spec["units_m"]
        if not isinstance(units_m, dict) or not units_m or any(
                not isinstance(f, (int, float)) or isinstance(f, bool) or not math.isfinite(f) or f <= 0
                for f in units_m.values()):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_units_invalid", source=name)
        _check_source_shape(name, fmt, spec)
        base = decl_dir if spec.get("base", _BASE_PDK_ROOT) == _BASE_DECL_DIR else root
        path = base.rstrip("/\\") + "/" + spec["path"]
        if not os.path.isfile(path):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_missing", source=name, path=path)
        with open(path, "rb") as fh:
            data = fh.read()
        if spec.get("newline") == _NEWLINE_LF:
            data = data.replace(_CRLF, _LF)
        digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        if digest != spec["sha256"]:
>           raise _reject("pdk_source_sha256_mismatch", source=name, path=path, declared=spec["sha256"], actual=digest)

    def _load_source(root, decl_dir, name, spec, memo):
        if not isinstance(spec, dict):
            raise _reject("pdk_decl_shape", where="sources." + name)
        fmt = spec.get("format")
        allowed = dict(_SOURCE_KEYS).get(fmt)
        if allowed is None:
            raise _reject("pdk_source_format_unknown", source=name, format=fmt)
        _require_keys("sources." + name, spec, allowed, tuple(k for k in allowed if k not in _OPTIONAL_SOURCE_KEYS))
        units_m = spec["units_m"]
        if not isinstance(units_m, dict) or not units_m or any(
                not isinstance(f, (int, float)) or isinstance(f, bool) or not math.isfinite(f) or f <= 0
                for f in units_m.values()):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_units_invalid", source=name)
        _check_source_shape(name, fmt, spec)
        base = decl_dir if spec.get("base", _BASE_PDK_ROOT) == _BASE_DECL_DIR else root
        path = base.rstrip("/\\") + "/" + spec["path"]
        if not os.path.isfile(path):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_missing", source=name, path=path)
        with open(path, "rb") as fh:
            data = fh.read()
        if spec.get("newline") == _NEWLINE_LF:
            data = data.replace(_CRLF, _LF)
        digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        if digest != spec["sha256"]:
>           raise _reject("pdk_source_sha256_mismatch", source=name, path=path, declared=spec["sha256"], actual=digest)

    def _load_source(root, decl_dir, name, spec, memo):
        if not isinstance(spec, dict):
            raise _reject("pdk_decl_shape", where="sources." + name)

        allowed = dict(_SOURCE_KEYS).get(fmt)
        if allowed is None:
            raise _reject("pdk_source_format_unknown", source=name, format=fmt)
        _require_keys("sources." + name, spec, allowed, tuple(k for k in allowed if k not in _OPTIONAL_SOURCE_KEYS))

                not isinstance(f, (int, float)) or isinstance(f, bool) or not math.isfinite(f) or f <= 0
                for f in units_m.values()):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_units_invalid", source=name)
        _check_source_shape(name, fmt, spec)
        base = decl_dir if spec.get("base", _BASE_PDK_ROOT) == _BASE_DECL_DIR else root
        path = base.rstrip("/\\") + "/" + spec["path"]
        if not os.path.isfile(path):

    def _load_source(root, decl_dir, name, spec, memo):
        if not isinstance(spec, dict):
            raise _reject("pdk_decl_shape", where="sources." + name)
        fmt = spec.get("format")
        allowed = dict(_SOURCE_KEYS).get(fmt)
        if allowed is None:
            raise _reject("pdk_source_format_unknown", source=name, format=fmt)
        _require_keys("sources." + name, spec, allowed, tuple(k for k in allowed if k not in _OPTIONAL_SOURCE_KEYS))
        units_m = spec["units_m"]
        if not isinstance(units_m, dict) or not units_m or any(
                not isinstance(f, (int, float)) or isinstance(f, bool) or not math.isfinite(f) or f <= 0
                for f in units_m.values()):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_units_invalid", source=name)
        _check_source_shape(name, fmt, spec)
        base = decl_dir if spec.get("base", _BASE_PDK_ROOT) == _BASE_DECL_DIR else root
        path = base.rstrip("/\\") + "/" + spec["path"]
        if not os.path.isfile(path):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_missing", source=name, path=path)
        with open(path, "rb") as fh:
            data = fh.read()
        if spec.get("newline") == _NEWLINE_LF:
            data = data.replace(_CRLF, _LF)
        digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        if digest != spec["sha256"]:
>           raise _reject("pdk_source_sha256_mismatch", source=name, path=path, declared=spec["sha256"], actual=digest)

        if allowed is None:

        base = decl_dir if spec.get("base", _BASE_PDK_ROOT) == _BASE_DECL_DIR else root

    def _load_source(root, decl_dir, name, spec, memo):
        if not isinstance(spec, dict):
            raise _reject("pdk_decl_shape", where="sources." + name)
        fmt = spec.get("format")
        allowed = dict(_SOURCE_KEYS).get(fmt)
        if allowed is None:
            raise _reject("pdk_source_format_unknown", source=name, format=fmt)
        _require_keys("sources." + name, spec, allowed, tuple(k for k in allowed if k not in _OPTIONAL_SOURCE_KEYS))
        units_m = spec["units_m"]
        if not isinstance(units_m, dict) or not units_m or any(
                not isinstance(f, (int, float)) or isinstance(f, bool) or not math.isfinite(f) or f <= 0
                for f in units_m.values()):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_units_invalid", source=name)
        _check_source_shape(name, fmt, spec)
        base = decl_dir if spec.get("base", _BASE_PDK_ROOT) == _BASE_DECL_DIR else root
        path = base.rstrip("/\\") + "/" + spec["path"]
        if not os.path.isfile(path):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_missing", source=name, path=path)
        with open(path, "rb") as fh:
            data = fh.read()
        if spec.get("newline") == _NEWLINE_LF:
            data = data.replace(_CRLF, _LF)
        digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        if digest != spec["sha256"]:

    def _load_source(root, decl_dir, name, spec, memo):
        if not isinstance(spec, dict):
            raise _reject("pdk_decl_shape", where="sources." + name)
        fmt = spec.get("format")
        allowed = dict(_SOURCE_KEYS).get(fmt)
        if allowed is None:
            raise _reject("pdk_source_format_unknown", source=name, format=fmt)
        _require_keys("sources." + name, spec, allowed, tuple(k for k in allowed if k not in _OPTIONAL_SOURCE_KEYS))
        units_m = spec["units_m"]
        if not isinstance(units_m, dict) or not units_m or any(
                not isinstance(f, (int, float)) or isinstance(f, bool) or not math.isfinite(f) or f <= 0
                for f in units_m.values()):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_units_invalid", source=name)
        _check_source_shape(name, fmt, spec)
        base = decl_dir if spec.get("base", _BASE_PDK_ROOT) == _BASE_DECL_DIR else root
        path = base.rstrip("/\\") + "/" + spec["path"]
        if not os.path.isfile(path):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_missing", source=name, path=path)
        with open(path, "rb") as fh:
            data = fh.read()
        if spec.get("newline") == _NEWLINE_LF:
            data = data.replace(_CRLF, _LF)
        digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        if digest != spec["sha256"]:
>           raise _reject("pdk_source_sha256_mismatch", source=name, path=path, declared=spec["sha256"], actual=digest)

    def _load_source(root, decl_dir, name, spec, memo):
        if not isinstance(spec, dict):
            raise _reject("pdk_decl_shape", where="sources." + name)
        fmt = spec.get("format")
        allowed = dict(_SOURCE_KEYS).get(fmt)
        if allowed is None:
            raise _reject("pdk_source_format_unknown", source=name, format=fmt)
        _require_keys("sources." + name, spec, allowed, tuple(k for k in allowed if k not in _OPTIONAL_SOURCE_KEYS))
        units_m = spec["units_m"]
        if not isinstance(units_m, dict) or not units_m or any(
                not isinstance(f, (int, float)) or isinstance(f, bool) or not math.isfinite(f) or f <= 0
                for f in units_m.values()):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_units_invalid", source=name)
        _check_source_shape(name, fmt, spec)
        base = decl_dir if spec.get("base", _BASE_PDK_ROOT) == _BASE_DECL_DIR else root
        path = base.rstrip("/\\") + "/" + spec["path"]
        if not os.path.isfile(path):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_missing", source=name, path=path)
        with open(path, "rb") as fh:

    def _load_source(root, decl_dir, name, spec, memo):
        if not isinstance(spec, dict):
            raise _reject("pdk_decl_shape", where="sources." + name)
        fmt = spec.get("format")
        allowed = dict(_SOURCE_KEYS).get(fmt)
        if allowed is None:
            raise _reject("pdk_source_format_unknown", source=name, format=fmt)
        _require_keys("sources." + name, spec, allowed, tuple(k for k in allowed if k not in _OPTIONAL_SOURCE_KEYS))
        units_m = spec["units_m"]
        if not isinstance(units_m, dict) or not units_m or any(
                not isinstance(f, (int, float)) or isinstance(f, bool) or not math.isfinite(f) or f <= 0
                for f in units_m.values()):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_units_invalid", source=name)
        _check_source_shape(name, fmt, spec)
        base = decl_dir if spec.get("base", _BASE_PDK_ROOT) == _BASE_DECL_DIR else root
        path = base.rstrip("/\\") + "/" + spec["path"]
        if not os.path.isfile(path):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_missing", source=name, path=path)
        with open(path, "rb") as fh:
            data = fh.read()
        if spec.get("newline") == _NEWLINE_LF:
            data = data.replace(_CRLF, _LF)
        digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        if digest != spec["sha256"]:

    def _load_source(root, decl_dir, name, spec, memo):
        if not isinstance(spec, dict):
            raise _reject("pdk_decl_shape", where="sources." + name)
        fmt = spec.get("format")
        allowed = dict(_SOURCE_KEYS).get(fmt)
        if allowed is None:
            raise _reject("pdk_source_format_unknown", source=name, format=fmt)
        _require_keys("sources." + name, spec, allowed, tuple(k for k in allowed if k not in _OPTIONAL_SOURCE_KEYS))
        units_m = spec["units_m"]
        if not isinstance(units_m, dict) or not units_m or any(
                not isinstance(f, (int, float)) or isinstance(f, bool) or not math.isfinite(f) or f <= 0
                for f in units_m.values()):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_units_invalid", source=name)
        _check_source_shape(name, fmt, spec)
        base = decl_dir if spec.get("base", _BASE_PDK_ROOT) == _BASE_DECL_DIR else root
        path = base.rstrip("/\\") + "/" + spec["path"]
        if not os.path.isfile(path):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_missing", source=name, path=path)
        with open(path, "rb") as fh:
            data = fh.read()
        if spec.get("newline") == _NEWLINE_LF:
            data = data.replace(_CRLF, _LF)
        digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        if digest != spec["sha256"]:
>           raise _reject("pdk_source_sha256_mismatch", source=name, path=path, declared=spec["sha256"], actual=digest)

    def _load_source(root, decl_dir, name, spec, memo):
        if not isinstance(spec, dict):
            raise _reject("pdk_decl_shape", where="sources." + name)
        fmt = spec.get("format")
        allowed = dict(_SOURCE_KEYS).get(fmt)
        if allowed is None:
            raise _reject("pdk_source_format_unknown", source=name, format=fmt)
        _require_keys("sources." + name, spec, allowed, tuple(k for k in allowed if k not in _OPTIONAL_SOURCE_KEYS))
        units_m = spec["units_m"]
        if not isinstance(units_m, dict) or not units_m or any(
                not isinstance(f, (int, float)) or isinstance(f, bool) or not math.isfinite(f) or f <= 0
                for f in units_m.values()):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_units_invalid", source=name)
        _check_source_shape(name, fmt, spec)
        base = decl_dir if spec.get("base", _BASE_PDK_ROOT) == _BASE_DECL_DIR else root
        path = base.rstrip("/\\") + "/" + spec["path"]
        if not os.path.isfile(path):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_missing", source=name, path=path)
        with open(path, "rb") as fh:
            data = fh.read()

    def _load_source(root, decl_dir, name, spec, memo):
        if not isinstance(spec, dict):
            raise _reject("pdk_decl_shape", where="sources." + name)
        fmt = spec.get("format")
        allowed = dict(_SOURCE_KEYS).get(fmt)
        if allowed is None:
            raise _reject("pdk_source_format_unknown", source=name, format=fmt)
        _require_keys("sources." + name, spec, allowed, tuple(k for k in allowed if k not in _OPTIONAL_SOURCE_KEYS))
        units_m = spec["units_m"]
        if not isinstance(units_m, dict) or not units_m or any(
                not isinstance(f, (int, float)) or isinstance(f, bool) or not math.isfinite(f) or f <= 0
                for f in units_m.values()):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_units_invalid", source=name)
        _check_source_shape(name, fmt, spec)
        base = decl_dir if spec.get("base", _BASE_PDK_ROOT) == _BASE_DECL_DIR else root
        path = base.rstrip("/\\") + "/" + spec["path"]
        if not os.path.isfile(path):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_missing", source=name, path=path)
        with open(path, "rb") as fh:
            data = fh.read()
        if spec.get("newline") == _NEWLINE_LF:
            data = data.replace(_CRLF, _LF)
        digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        if digest != spec["sha256"]:
>           raise _reject("pdk_source_sha256_mismatch", source=name, path=path, declared=spec["sha256"], actual=digest)

    def _load_source(root, decl_dir, name, spec, memo):

        if allowed is None:
            raise _reject("pdk_source_format_unknown", source=name, format=fmt)
        _require_keys("sources." + name, spec, allowed, tuple(k for k in allowed if k not in _OPTIONAL_SOURCE_KEYS))
        units_m = spec["units_m"]
        if not isinstance(units_m, dict) or not units_m or any(
                not isinstance(f, (int, float)) or isinstance(f, bool) or not math.isfinite(f) or f <= 0
                for f in units_m.values()):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_units_invalid", source=name)

        rho = env["pdk"].value("mim_cap_density_f_per_m2")
        want = {}
        for e in _topo(circuit).get("passive_elements", []):
            if e["kind"] != "capacitor":
                continue
            decl = ev.get(e["name"], {})
            if isinstance(decl, dict) and decl.get("measurement_load"):
                continue
            if isinstance(decl, dict) and "value_farad" in decl:
                want[e["name"]] = decl["value_farad"] / rho
            elif isinstance(theta.get(e["name"]), dict) and "min" in theta[e["name"]]:
                want[e["name"]] = theta[e["name"]]["min"] / rho
        st = env["stats"]
        for mos, (kind, src) in _CURRENT_FED.get(circuit, {}).items():
            want[mos] = ev[src]["value_dc_amp"] * st["l_min"][kind] ** 2 / st["idu_max"][kind]
        got = {k: v for k, v in area["terms"]}

    def _load_source(root, decl_dir, name, spec, memo):
        if not isinstance(spec, dict):
            raise _reject("pdk_decl_shape", where="sources." + name)
        fmt = spec.get("format")
        allowed = dict(_SOURCE_KEYS).get(fmt)
        if allowed is None:
            raise _reject("pdk_source_format_unknown", source=name, format=fmt)
        _require_keys("sources." + name, spec, allowed, tuple(k for k in allowed if k not in _OPTIONAL_SOURCE_KEYS))
        units_m = spec["units_m"]
        if not isinstance(units_m, dict) or not units_m or any(
                not isinstance(f, (int, float)) or isinstance(f, bool) or not math.isfinite(f) or f <= 0
                for f in units_m.values()):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_units_invalid", source=name)
        _check_source_shape(name, fmt, spec)
        base = decl_dir if spec.get("base", _BASE_PDK_ROOT) == _BASE_DECL_DIR else root
        path = base.rstrip("/\\") + "/" + spec["path"]
        if not os.path.isfile(path):
            raise _reject("pdk_source_missing", source=name, path=path)
        with open(path, "rb") as fh:
            data = fh.read()
        if spec.get("newline") == _NEWLINE_LF:
            data = data.replace(_CRLF, _LF)
        digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
        if digest != spec["sha256"]:
>           raise _reject("pdk_source_sha256_mismatch", source=name, path=path, declared=spec["sha256"], actual=digest)
